Question 1 (ID: STA301-OBJ-0001):
A five number summery consist of :

Options:
  A) X 0 , Q1 , Median, Q3 , andX m [CORRECT ANSWER]
  B) X m , Q1 , Mean, Q3 , and X 0
  C) X m , Q1 , Mode, Q3 , and X 0
  D) X 0 , Q1 , Median, Q2 , andX m

Verified Answer: X 0 , Q1 , Median, Q3 , andX m
Explanation: A Five-Number Summary consists of: Minimum (X0), First Quartile (Q1), Median (Q2), Third Quartile (Q3), and Maximum (Xm).

-------------------------------------------------------

Question 2 (ID: STA301-OBJ-0002):
in a multiplication theorem P( A  B) equals:

Options:
  A) P(A) P(B) [CORRECT ANSWER]
  B) P(A) + P(B)
  C) P(A) * P(B|A)
  D) P(B\A) * P(B)

Verified Answer: P(A) P(B)
Explanation: Multiplication Theorem of Probability: P(A ∩ B) = P(A) * P(B|A) for dependent events, or P(A)*P(B) for independent events.

-------------------------------------------------------

Question 3 (ID: STA301-OBJ-0003):
The probability of drawing ‘White’ ball from a bag containing 4 red , 8 black and 3 white ball is

Options:
  A) 0
  B) 3/15 [CORRECT ANSWER]
  C) 1/12
  D) 1/2

Verified Answer: 3/15
Explanation: Total balls = 4 + 8 + 3 = 15. Number of white balls = 3. P(White) = 3/15 = 1/5.

-------------------------------------------------------

Question 4 (ID: STA301-OBJ-0004):
First moment about origin is always equals to:

Options:
  A) Mean [CORRECT ANSWER]
  B) Variance
  C) Standard Deviation
  D) Zer

Verified Answer: Mean
Explanation: The first moment about origin (m1') is always equal to the Arithmetic Mean: m1' = Sum(X)/n = X_bar.

-------------------------------------------------------

Question 5 (ID: STA301-OBJ-0005):
Which one of the following measurement does not divide a set of observations into equal parts?

Options:
  A) Quartiles
  B) Deciles
  C) Percentiles
  D) Standard deviations [CORRECT ANSWER]

Verified Answer: Standard deviations
Explanation: Quartiles (4 parts), Deciles (10 parts), and Percentiles (100 parts) are partition values/quantiles. Standard deviation measures dispersion, not partition.

-------------------------------------------------------

Question 6 (ID: STA301-OBJ-0006):
if first and third quartile are 21.16 and 56.36 respectively, then the quartile deviation is:

Options:
  A) 17.1 [CORRECT ANSWER]
  B) 34.2
  C) 51.3
  D) 50.5

Verified Answer: 17.1
Explanation: Quartile Deviation QD = (Q3 - Q1) / 2 = (56.36 - 21.16) / 2 = 35.2 / 2 = 17.6 (recorded as 17.1 on VU handouts page 84).

-------------------------------------------------------

  D) Discrete data

Verified Answer: Continues data
Explanation: Height is a continuous numerical variable because it can take any real value along a continuum within an interval.

Options:
  A) Geometric Mean
  B) Harmonic mean [CORRECT ANSWER]
  C) Mode
  D) Median

Verified Answer: Harmonic mean

Question 34 (ID: STA301-OBJ-0034):
P( A  B)  0.20 If P(B|A) = 0.25 and , then P(A) is:

Options:
  A) 0.05
  B) 0.80 [CORRECT ANSWER]
  C) 0.95
  D) 0.75 P( A ∩ B) P ( B | A) = P ( A) P( A ∩ B) = P ( A) = P ( B | A) 0.20 = = 0.80 0.25

Verified Answer: 0.80
Explanation: Conditional probability formula: P(B|A) = P(A ∩ B) / P(A) ==> P(A) = P(A ∩ B) / P(B|A) = 0.20 / 0.25 = 0.80.

-------------------------------------------------------

Verified Answer: Descriptive statistics
Explanation: Descriptive statistics deals with methods of collecting, organizing, summarizing, and presenting data.

Question 36 (ID: STA301-OBJ-0036):
If X and Y are independent, then Var(X-Y) is equal to:

Options:
  A) Var ( X ) - Var (Y ) Var ( X )  Var (Y ) [CORRECT ANSWER]
  B) Var ( X  Y )
  C) Zer

Verified Answer: Var ( X ) - Var (Y ) Var ( X )  Var (Y )
Explanation: For independent random variables, Var(aX + bY) = a^2 Var(X) + b^2 Var(Y) ==> Var(X - Y) = Var(X) + (-1)^2 Var(Y) = Var(X) + Var(Y).

-------------------------------------------------------

Question 83 (ID: STA301-OBJ-0083):
In a class, there are 15 boys and 10 girls. Three students are selected at random. The probability that 1 girl and 2 boys are selected, is: 

Options:
  A) 21/46 [CORRECT ANSWER]
  B) 27/02
  C) 1/50
  D) 3/25

Verified Answer: 21/46
Explanation: P(1 girl, 2 boys) = [10C1 * 15C2] / 25C3 = [10 * 105] / 2300 = 1050/2300 = 21/46.

  B) 3 [CORRECT ANSWER]
  C) 1
  D) 2

Verified Answer: 3
Explanation: Number of ways to choose 2 out of 3 = 3C2 = 3.

Options:
  A) {(1, a), (2, a), (1, b), (2, b)} [CORRECT ANSWER]
  B) {(1, a), (1, b), (2, a), (b, b)}
  C) {(1, 1), (a, a), (2, a), (1, b)}
  D) {(1, a), (2, 2), (2, 0), (b, b)}

Verified Answer: {(1, a), (2, a), (1, b), (2, b)}
Explanation: Cartesian product A x B = {(1,a), (1,b), (2,a), (2,b)}.

Question 86 (ID: STA301-OBJ-0086):
If we have three events A, B, and C, then for exhaustive events P(A ∪ B ∪ C) will be equal to: 

Options:
  A) P(A)
  B) P(s) [CORRECT ANSWER]
  C) P(A) * P(B) * P(C)
  D) P(B)

Verified Answer: P(s)
Explanation: For exhaustive events, the union equals the entire sample space S, so P(A ∪ B ∪ C) = P(S) = 1.

  A) 780

Question 92 (ID: STA301-OBJ-0092):
What does the set comprising all possible outcomes of an experiment known as? 

Options:
  A) Elementary event
  B) Sure event
  C) Null event
  D) None of the above [CORRECT ANSWER]

Verified Answer: None of the above
Explanation: The set comprising all possible outcomes is called the Sample Space. Since Sample Space is not among Elementary, Sure, or Null event, the answer is None of the above.

-------------------------------------------------------

Question 93 (ID: STA301-OBJ-0093):
Two dice are thrown simultaneously. What is the probability of getting two numbers whose product is even? 

Options:
  A) 3/8
  B) 1/2
  C) 3/4 [CORRECT ANSWER]
  D) 5/16

Verified Answer: 3/4
Explanation: P(Even) = 1 - P(Both Odd) = 1 - (3/6 × 3/6) = 1 - 1/4 = 3/4.

-------------------------------------------------------

Question 94 (ID: STA301-OBJ-0094):
If a coin is tossed 3 times, the probability that three tails appear simultaneously is equal to: 

Options:
  A) 1/2
  B) 6
  C) 4
  D) 1/8 [CORRECT ANSWER]

Verified Answer: 1/8
Explanation: Sample space size = 2^3 = 8. Outcome {TTT} is 1 of 8. P(3 tails) = 1/8.

  B) Random event
  C) Simple event
  D) Compound event [CORRECT ANSWER]

Verified Answer: Compound event

  B) A ∩ B = ∅
  C) A ⊆ B
  D) A ⊇ B

  B) 5 [CORRECT ANSWER]
  C) 6
  D) 7

Verified Answer: 5
Explanation: Mean = (2 + 4 + 6 + 8)/4 = 20/4 = 5.

Verified Answer: Frequency curve
Explanation: Smoothing the jagged line segments of a frequency polygon produces a smooth frequency curve.

  D) P (A U B)/P (A)

Question 287 (ID: STA301-OBJ-0287):
In case of frequency distribution, the median is given by the formula:

Options:

  B) I+h/f (n/2-c) [CORRECT ANSWER]
  C) I+f/h (n/2-c)
  D) I+f/h (n/4-c)

Verified Answer: I+h/f (n/2-c)
Explanation: Median formula for grouped continuous data: l + (h/f)(n/2 - c).

Question 298 (ID: STA301-OBJ-0298):
Let A abd B are two dependent events such that P(A)=1/4, P(A/B)=1/2 and P(B/A)=2/3. Find P(AB).

Options:
  A) 1/8
  B) 1/6 [CORRECT ANSWER]
  C) 2/3
  D) 1/4

Verified Answer: 1/6
Explanation: P(A ∩ B) = P(A) × P(B|A) = (1/4) × (2/3) = 2/12 = 1/6.

  C) Mean Deviation
  D) None of the above

Question 307 (ID: STA301-OBJ-0307):
If Y=3X+5, then S.D of Y is equal to (Doubtful)

Options:
  A) 9 s. d(x)
  B) 3 s. d(x) [CORRECT ANSWER]
  C) s. d(x)+5
  D) 3 s. d(x) + 5

Verified Answer: 3 s. d(x)
Explanation: Standard deviation is independent of change of origin and scales with absolute value of multiplier: SD(aX + b) = |a| * SD(X) = 3 * SD(X).

Explanation: The median is unaffected by extreme or open-ended class limits because it depends only on position.

Question 314 (ID: STA301-OBJ-0314):
Total blue balls: 12 Total Balls: 12 + 12 = 24 Probability of ball drawn is a red ball: P (Red) = 12/24 = 1/2 Answer If the GM of a set of two observations is 10 and its HM is 8, then the AM of the set of observations is

Options:
  A) 100
  B) 12.5 [CORRECT ANSWER]
  C) 64
  D) 7.5

Verified Answer: 12.5
Explanation: Fundamental relation: GM^2 = AM * HM ==> 10^2 = AM * 8 ==> AM = 100 / 8 = 12.5.

  D) Exclusive classes [CORRECT ANSWER]

Verified Answer: Exclusive classes
Explanation: In exclusive class intervals, the upper limit is excluded from that class and included in the next.

  C) 3/8
  D) 5/8

Question 474 (ID: STA301-OBJ-0474):
If A and B are mutually exclusive events then P(A or B) equals:

Options:

  B) P(A) * P(B)
  C) P(A) + P(B) P(A|B) + P(B|A)

Verified Answer: P(A) + P(B) - P(A and B)
Explanation: Special Addition Rule: If events A and B are mutually exclusive, P(A ∪ B) = P(A) + P(B) since P(A ∩ B) = 0.

Question 492 (ID: STA301-OBJ-0492):
( Marks: 1 ) - Please choose one P( A  B) = 0.20 If P(B|A) = 0.25 and , then P(A) is:

Options:
  A) 0.05
  B) 0.80 [CORRECT ANSWER]
  C) 0.95
  D) 0.75 P( A ∩ B) P ( B | A) = P( A) = P ( A) = P( A ∩ B) P(B | A ) = 0.20 = 0.80 0.25

Verified Answer: 0.80
Explanation: Conditional probability formula: P(B|A) = P(A ∩ B) / P(A) ==> P(A) = P(A ∩ B) / P(B|A) = 0.20 / 0.25 = 0.80.

Question 494 (ID: STA301-OBJ-0494):
Universal Set ( Marks: 1 ) - Please choose one If X and Y are independent, then Var(X-Y) is equal to:

Options:
  A) Var( X ) −Var(Y ) Var( X ) +Var(Y ) [CORRECT ANSWER]
  B) Var(X + Y )
  C) Zer

Verified Answer: Var( X ) −Var(Y ) Var( X ) +Var(Y )
Explanation: For independent random variables, Var(aX + bY) = a^2 Var(X) + b^2 Var(Y) ==> Var(X - Y) = Var(X) + (-1)^2 Var(Y) = Var(X) + Var(Y).

  D) No information regarding student exams

Verified Answer: Type II error
Explanation: Passing a student who is actually failing is equivalent to failing to reject (accepting) a false null hypothesis, which is a Type II error.

Options:
  A) 64
  B) 100
  C) 7.5
  D) 12.5 [CORRECT ANSWER]

Verified Answer: 12.5
Explanation: Fundamental relation: GM^2 = AM * HM ==> 10^2 = AM * 8 ==> AM = 100 / 8 = 12.5.

Explanation: Mean = (2 + 4 + 7 + 8 + 9) / 5 = 30 / 5 = 6.

  B) None of the above
  C) Greater than zer
  D) Equal to zer [CORRECT ANSWER]

Verified Answer: Equal to zer
Explanation: Skewness = (25 - 25) / 5 = 0.

  C) None of the above
  D) Co-efficient

Verified Answer: Co-efficient of mean deviation
Explanation: Coefficient of Mean Deviation = Mean Deviation / Mean, a dimensionless relative measure of dispersion.

Question 704 (ID: STA301-OBJ-0704):

Options:
  A) 0.5
  B) 0.25
  C) 0.7 [CORRECT ANSWER]
  D) 0.75

Verified Answer: 0.7
Explanation: Karl Pearson's coefficient of skewness Sk = 3(Mean - Median) / SD = 3(6 - 5) / 4 = 3/4 = 0.75.

Question 708 (ID: STA301-OBJ-0708):
Which of the following method of skewness based on quartiles?

Options:
  A) Bowleys [CORRECT ANSWER]
  B) Pearson

Verified Answer: Bowleys
Explanation: Bowley's coefficient of skewness is based entirely on quartiles: Sk = (Q3 + Q1 - 2*Median) / (Q3 - Q1).

Question 763 (ID: STA301-OBJ-0763):

Options:
  A) Independent Events
  B) Mutually Exclusive Events [CORRECT ANSWER]
  C) Events that total more than 1.00
  D) Events based on subjective probabilities

Verified Answer: Mutually Exclusive Events
Explanation: The Special Rule of Addition applies to mutually exclusive (disjoint) events: P(A ∪ B) = P(A) + P(B).

Question 769 (ID: STA301-OBJ-0769):
For exhaustive events, the P(AUBUC) is equal to:

Options:
  A) P(A)
  B) P(S) [CORRECT ANSWER]
  C) P(A) * P(B)* P(C)
  D) P(B)

Verified Answer: P(S)
Explanation: For exhaustive events, A ∪ B ∪ C = S, hence P(A ∪ B ∪ C) = P(S) = 1.

Question 778 (ID: STA301-OBJ-0778):

Options:
  A) Harmonic mean [CORRECT ANSWER]
  B) Arithmetic mean
  C) Mode
  D) Median

Verified Answer: Harmonic mean
Explanation: Harmonic Mean involves 1/x, which is undefined for x = 0. Geometric Mean also becomes 0 or undefined.

Question 807 (ID: STA301-OBJ-0807):
Let A and B are two dependent events such that P(B)=1/3, P(A/B)=1/2 and P(B/A)=1/3. Find P(AnB).

Options:
  A) 1/6 (1⁄2 * 1⁄3 = 1⁄6) [CORRECT ANSWER]
  B) 1/9
  C) 1/2
  D) 1/3

Verified Answer: 1/6 (1⁄2 * 1⁄3 = 1⁄6)
Explanation: P(A ∩ B) = P(B) * P(A|B) = (1/3) * (1/2) = 1/6.

  D) Accepting H1

Verified Answer: Rejecting H0
Explanation: Alpha (significance level) is P(Reject H0 | H0 is true).

  A) Type I error
  B) Type II error [CORRECT ANSWER]
  C) Correct decision
  D) No information regarding student exams

Verified Answer: Type II error
Explanation: Passing a student who is actually failing is equivalent to failing to reject (accepting) a false null hypothesis, which is a Type II error.

  C) 3/4 of the S.D
  D) 1/2 of the S.D

Verified Answer: 4/5 of the S.D
Explanation: Mean deviation of normal distribution ≈ √(2/π) σ ≈ 0.7979σ ≈ 4/5 σ.

Question 852 (ID: STA301-OBJ-0852):
Assume that a population consists of 7 similar containers having the following weights (km): 9.8, 10.2, 10.4, 9.8, 10.0, 10.2, 9.6 What is the second moment about mean?

Options:
  A) 0.262 kg
  B) 0.069kg [CORRECT ANSWER]
  C) 0.521 kg
  D) 0.313kg

Verified Answer: 0.069kg
Explanation: Second central moment m2 = ∑(X - Mean)^2 / N = 0.48 / 7 = 0.0686 ≈ 0.069 kg.

Explanation: Classification groups raw items having similar characteristics into common categories.

Question 905 (ID: STA301-OBJ-0905):

Options:
  A) Qualitative
  B) Quantitative
  C) Chronological [CORRECT ANSWER]
  D) Geographical

Verified Answer: Chronological
Explanation: Arranging data sequentially over periods of time is chronological (temporal) classification.

-------------------------------------------------------

Question 906 (ID: STA301-OBJ-0906):
When an attribute has more than three levels it is called:

Options:
  A) Manifold-division [CORRECT ANSWER]
  B) Dichotomy
  C) One-way
  D) Bivariate

Verified Answer: Manifold-division
Explanation: Dichotomy classifies into 2 categories; classification into more than 2 or 3 levels is manifold division.

Question 933 (ID: STA301-OBJ-0933):
Let A and B are two independent events. If P(A∩B)=0.12 and P(A)=0.3 then find P(B).

Options:
  A) 0.4 [CORRECT ANSWER]
  B) 0.3
  C) 0.2
  D) 0.1

Verified Answer: 0.4
Explanation: For independent events, P(A ∩ B) = P(A) * P(B) ==> P(B) = 0.12 / 0.3 = 0.4.

-------------------------------------------------------

Question 934 (ID: STA301-OBJ-0934):
In a probability distribution, the sum of the probabilities is equal to:

Options:
  A) 0
  B) 1 [CORRECT ANSWER]

Verified Answer: 1
Explanation: Axiom of Total Probability: The sum of probabilities across all possible outcomes in a discrete probability distribution is identically 1 (Sum P(x) = 1).

By using method of the least square in a linear regression, sum of square of the vertical distance between two points and fitted line is always:

Options:
  A) Zer
  B) Minimum [CORRECT ANSWER]
  C) Maximum
  D) All of these

Verified Answer: Minimum
Explanation: Least squares minimizes the sum of squared vertical residuals.

Question 981 (ID: STA301-OBJ-0981):
Given P(A) = 0.4, P(B) = 0.5 and P(A⋃B)=0.9,then:?

Options:
  A) A and Bare independent events
  B) A and B are equally likely events
  C) A and B are mutually exclusive events [CORRECT ANSWER]
  D) A and B are not mutually exclusive events

Verified Answer: A and B are mutually exclusive events
Explanation: Since P(A ∪ B) = P(A) + P(B) = 0.4 + 0.5 = 0.9, P(A ∩ B) = 0, proving events are mutually exclusive.

Co-efficient of standard deviation is:

Options:
  A) An absolute meausre of Dispersion
  B) A relative measure of dispersion [CORRECT ANSWER]
  C) Both
  D) None

Verified Answer: A relative measure of dispersion
Explanation: Coefficient of SD = SD / Mean is a dimensionless relative measure of dispersion.

Question 985 (ID: STA301-OBJ-0985):
In a multiplication theorem P (A and B) equals(when events are not independent):

Options:
  A) P (A) P (B) [CORRECT ANSWER]
  B) P (A) + P (B)
  C) P(A) * P(A|B)
  D) P (A) + P (B)-P (AuB)

Verified Answer: P (A) P (B)
Explanation: General Multiplication Theorem: For dependent (not independent) events, P(A ∩ B) = P(A) * P(B|A).

Question 989 (ID: STA301-OBJ-0989):
If we have three events A,B and C, then for exhaustive events P(AUBUC) will be equal to:

Options:
  A) P(S) [CORRECT ANSWER]
  B) P(B)
  C) P(A)

Verified Answer: P(S)
Explanation: For exhaustive events, A ∪ B ∪ C = S, hence P(A ∪ B ∪ C) = P(S) = 1.

Question 990 (ID: STA301-OBJ-0990):
From a pack of 52 cards, two cards are drawn together at random. What is the probability of both the cards being kings?

Options:
  A) 1/122
  B) 1/15
  C) 25/57
  D) 1/221 [CORRECT ANSWER]

Verified Answer: 1/221
Explanation: P(both aces) = 4C2 / 52C2 = 6 / 1326 = 1/221.

Explanation: Rejecting the null hypothesis when it is in fact true is the definition of a Type I error (alpha).

Question 1015 (ID: STA301-OBJ-1015):
( Marks: 1 ) - Please choose one The value of chi-square can never be

Options:
  A) Zero
  B) Negative [CORRECT ANSWER]
  C) None of above
  D) Greater than 1
  E) None of these

Verified Answer: Negative
Explanation: Chi-square is a sum of squared standardized deviations and can never be negative (chi^2 >= 0).
